# Fix Registry

> Master fix ledger for the Quant OS project.
> Each fix has a unique ID, root cause category, and prevention measure.

## Fix ID Format

```
FIX-YYYYMMDD-NNN
```
- YYYYMMDD: date fix was applied
- NNN: sequential counter per day (001, 002, ...)

## Root Cause Categories

| Code | Category | Description |
|------|----------|-------------|
| RC-01 | missing-null-check | None/empty not handled |
| RC-02 | type-confusion | Wrong type passed or assumed |
| RC-03 | state-leak | State from prior cycle bleeding through |
| RC-04 | race-condition | Concurrent access without synchronization |
| RC-05 | boundary-error | Off-by-one, edge case, range error |
| RC-06 | contract-violation | Interface contract not honored |
| RC-07 | missing-validation | Input not validated at boundary |
| RC-08 | incomplete-cleanup | Resources not released, listeners not removed |
| RC-09 | config-drift | Configuration inconsistent with code |
| RC-10 | dependency-order | Initialization/teardown order wrong |
| RC-11 | stale-data | Artifacts/configs from deprecated/retired models not cleaned up |
| RC-12 | missing-feature | Required capability not yet implemented |
| RC-13 | stale-data-artifact | State file not updated to reflect code/schema changes |
| RC-14 | ad-hoc-query | System status queried via one-liner without dedup/validation (Iron Law #11 violation) |

| FIX-20260710-004 | 2026-07-10 | runtime, trail-dispatch | **L3: Trailing TP + SL STOPLEVEL violation — MT5 "Invalid stops" rejection loop, two-phase fix** (DQAF-20260710-004, Sev 2). Phase 1 (TP gate): `compute_trail_tp()` derived TP from entry_price + ATR distance — agnostic to current market price. When price advanced toward the original TP, the trailing TP candidate fell inside MT5's minimum stop distance from bid/ask, causing MT5 to reject the entire `modify_sltp` with retcode 10016. Phase 2 (SL gate, post-restart observation): the Chandelier trailing SL was ALSO vulnerable — after a price pullback, the computed SL could end up on the wrong side of current market price (LONG SL=4108.01 > bid=4106.47 on #4118779584; #4118881082 same pattern), triggering the same 10016 rejection loop. The entry-price anchor never changes, so the same invalid SL/TP was recomputed every cycle. Fix: `trail_dispatch.py:compute_and_dispatch_trail()` now validates BOTH SL and TP against current market price before dispatch. TP gate: LONG `TP > bid + min_distance`, SHORT `TP < ask − min_distance`. SL gate: LONG `SL < bid − min_distance`, SHORT `SL > ask + min_distance`. Infeasible stops revert to `pos.current_sl`/`pos.current_tp` (NOT 0.0 — prevents state corruption where `pos.current_*` would be optimistically zeroed at L335). No-op dispatch guard skips modify_sltp when both SL and TP were reverted (no effective change). `_spread` and `_min_market_distance` hoisted to shared scope. Diagnostic tags: `tp_released_stoplevel_violation`, `sl_released_stoplevel_violation`. | FIX-20260713-008 | 2026-07-13 | execution-orders | **L2: TP trailing structural parity — SL-Anchored TP + Proximity Gate + TP Floor + Debounce**. The pre-008 TP trail was price-blind and structurally asymmetric to the SL trail: ``compute_trail_tp()`` anchored solely to ``entry_price`` with a single trigger (ATR ≤ 0.80), while the Chandelier SL trail was price-aware (``highest_high/lowest_low``) with 5 protection layers. Four-phase fix: **(1) Dynamic SL Anchor**: TP anchor follows ``pos.current_sl`` — which already tracks price peaks via Chandelier. Pre-breakeven (SL ≤ entry): anchor = entry_price (legacy). Post-breakeven (SL > entry): anchor = current_sl — TP rides the same price-sensing rail as the SL, structurally preventing Bracket Inversion (FIX-20260707-009). **(2) Proximity Gate**: when price has covered ≥ ``tp_proximity_ratio`` of entry→current-TP journey, TP tightening is suppressed — the runner is in the home stretch. **(3) TP Floor**: ``tp_min_distance_atr × bracket_atr`` minimum from anchor — structural counterpart to SL's ``max_lock_atr`` ceiling. **(4) Min-step Debounce**: ``tp_min_step`` guards MT5 NO_CHANGES (10025). **2026-07-13 activation (全盤激活)**: TrailPolicy defaults changed from 0.0→active (0.7/1.5/0.15); ``compute_trail_tp`` fallback chained to engine ``default_policy`` (covers positions without explicit ``trail_policy``); **SHORT TP floor bugfix**: ``min()→max()`` — floor was silently inoperative on SHORT positions (default 0.0 masked the bug). Set individual fields to 0.0 on strategy ``TrailPolicy`` to opt out. 9/9 + 123/123 trail tests green. ReB: ``TP_TRAIL_PRICE_BLIND_ANCHOR_ASYMMETRY``. | L2 — design asymmetry: SL trail price-aware with 5-layer protection, TP trail price-blind with no protection beyond profitability gate |

## Fix Details by Quarter

> Fix detail entries are stored in quarterly files under `fixes/`.
> New entries: append to the current quarter's file via `register_fix.py`.
> The Fix Index table above is the central lookup — it is updated with every fix.

| Quarter | Period | File | Approx Entries |
|---------|--------|------|----------------|
| 2026 Q2 | Apr–Jun 2026 | [fixes/FIX_2026_Q2.md](fixes/FIX_2026_Q2.md) | 212 |
| 2026 Q3 | Jul–Sep 2026 | fixes/FIX_2026_Q3.md | — |
| 2026 Q4 | Oct–Dec 2026 | fixes/FIX_2026_Q4.md | — |

### Quarter File Template

New quarterly files are created from `fixes/_TEMPLATE.md`. When a new quarter begins:
1. Copy `_TEMPLATE.md` → `FIX_YYYY_QN.md`
2. Update this table
3. `register_fix.py` auto-detects quarter from date and writes to the correct file

### Deprecated: Year File

The previous `FIX_REGISTRY_2026.md` year-file design was never populated — all entries
stayed inline in this file. The 2026-05-22 to 2026-06-25 entries have been migrated to
`fixes/FIX_2026_Q2.md` as the initial population.
